# STEA-004 / Next Implementation Session
## Jira -> ACMS -> Agent -> Jira Recovery FIRST, then Internal MCP Gateway Build

**Date:** 2026-10-02  
**Executor:** STEA-004 Entrepreneur or replacement implementation agent  
**Primary execution model:** Prefer GPT-5.6 Sol at High/Max reasoning if available  
**Primary rule:** Do not begin MCP implementation until the existing Jira -> ACMS -> worker -> artifact -> Jira loop is proven working again with STNA-88.

# 0. BLUF

The ACMS/A2A production path was previously proven end-to-end. Phase A completed backend hygiene, Phase B completed IDs/handoffs/artifacts/repository metrics/power APIs/search, and Phase C completed the operator UI and final production demonstration.

A new real-world test now fails:

```text
Jira STNA-88
status = TO START
assignee = Startup Teams
human clicks "Check Jira now"
ACMS says reconciliation run completed
BUT:
  no Work Item appears
  no worker executes
  no handoff/artifact appears
  no Jira execution notes appear
  requested ACMS version change does not happen
```

Therefore:

```text
PRIORITY 0:
RESTORE AND PROVE JIRA -> ACMS -> AGENT -> JIRA
```

Only after that proof passes:

```text
PRIORITY 1:
BUILD INTERNAL MCP GATEWAY MVP
```

Approved MCP shape:

```text
Internal AI Agent
      |
      | MCP
      v
https://mcp.miam.home.arpa/
      |
      +-- acms.*
      +-- llm.*
      +-- runtime.*
      +-- proxmox.*
      +-- github.*
      +-- pdu.*
      +-- power.*
      +-- jira.*
      +-- registry.*
      +-- monitoring.*
```

The gateway is one logical endpoint, but not one giant root-access service. Domain services keep authority. Existing REST/A2A/SSE stay in place.

Do not rely on a name like "GPT-5.6 Turbo" as the canonical OpenAI model identifier.

```text
GPT-5.6 Sol
reasoning = High or Max
```

Use plan files, checkpoints, tests, and live evidence as authority. Model choice must not alter requirements.

# 4. STOP GATE

No MCP code until this passes:

```text
STNA-88
-> ACMS Jira reconcile
-> Work Item created
-> Work UID assigned
-> Project mapped
-> worker selected
-> assignment ACK
-> RUNNING
-> agent changes ACMS version number
-> tests
-> safe deployment

-> Jira BLUF comment
-> exact artifact URL
-> Jira transition through policy
```

If this cannot be proven, STOP and write a Jira-workflow diagnostic handoff.

# 5. Window 0 - Diagnose STNA-88

Read STNA-88 directly and record:

```text
issue key
project
issue type
assignee account ID/display
status
labels
description
template fields
created/updated
sprint
product/project fields
```

Find the exact reconciliation run created by "Check Jira now" and record:

```text
run ID
JQL/query
issues returned
issues inspected
issues accepted
issues rejected
issues skipped
issues updated
errors
per-issue reason
```

A reconcile run must never silently skip a candidate issue.

Every candidate needs a durable outcome:

```text
IMPORTED
ALREADY_EXISTS
NOT_ELIGIBLE
ASSIGNEE_MISMATCH
STATUS_MISMATCH
MISSING_REQUIRED_CONTEXT
INVALID_TEMPLATE
PROJECT_MAPPING_FAILED
ERROR
```

Compare STNA-88 against STNA-87, STNA-86, current Jira SOP, and current config.

Investigate, do not guess:

```text
assignee account mismatch
status normalization
project/issue-type filters
template validation
missing Product/Project
labels
Sprint assumptions
JQL regression
TO START normalization
pagination
ADF parsing
dedupe false positive
clock/time filter
state-machine regression
```

If current code cannot explain the skip, add instrumentation first.

The UI should show, for example:

```text
Run <id>
Scanned 4
Imported 1
Already known 1
Skipped 2

STNA-88 -> NOT_ELIGIBLE
Reason: <specific reason>
```

# 6. Fix Jira Intake

Patch the root cause only.

Preserve:

```text
manual Check Jira now
scheduled poll
idempotency
one Jira issue -> one Work Item
durable existing links
```

Do not broadly accept every TO START issue.

# 7. STNA-88 Production Acceptance

STNA-88 is the mandatory proof.

Expected:

```text
STNA-88
-> exactly one ACMS Work Item
-> ACMS-WORK-######-YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS
-> Project = ACMS or mapped equivalent
-> real A2A ACK
-> actual execution
```

Prefer worker uid-005 unless scheduler validly chooses another.

Agent must:

```text
read STNA-88
find canonical version source
change version correctly
run tests
commit/PR using repo policy
deploy safely
verify homepage shows new version
```

Do not fake success by changing display text only if version has a canonical application source.

Completion creates exactly one canonical handoff artifact:

```text
ACMS-ARTIFACT-######-YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS
```

Jira receives:

```text
short BLUF
Work UID
Artifact UID
specific artifact URL
production proof
```

Transitions through ACMS policy:

```text
TO START -> IN PROGRESS -> IN REVIEW
```

Blocked may transition to BLOCKED.

Reviewer/authorized role may later do:

```text
IN REVIEW -> COMPLETED
```

Take screenshots/API evidence of the whole path.

# 9. MCP Architecture - Approved Direction

After Jira acceptance passes:

```text
                         INTERNAL AGENTS ONLY
                               |
                               | MCP
                               v
                 https://mcp.miam.home.arpa/
                               |
                    +--------------------+
                    | Thin MCP Gateway   |
                    | VM114              |
                    | logically separate |
                    +--------------------+
                               |
       +-----------------------+-----------------------+
       |           |           |          |            |
       v           v           v          v            v
     ACMS         LLM       Runtime     GitHub       Jira
    adapter      adapter     adapter     adapter      adapter
       |
       +---- Proxmox / PDU / Emporia / Registry / Monitoring
```

Gateway responsibilities:

```text
authenticate
authorize
discover capabilities
route tool/resource calls
validate scopes
log activity
normalize errors
```

Gateway does NOT:

```text
replace ACMS
replace LLM Manager
replace Server Manager
replace Jira
replace GitHub
replace PDU Manager
own domain business logic
provide root shell to everything
```

# 10. Host / DNS / Exposure

Initial deployment:

```text
VM114
service = miam-mcp-gateway
DNS = mcp.miam.home.arpa
```

Internal/Tailscale only.

No public exposure.

Use existing internal TLS/CA pattern.

# 11. Internal Agents Only

This gateway is for Startup Teams internal managed agents.

External/customer agents are out of scope and must later use a separate gateway/policy boundary with stricter context isolation.

# 12. Agent Bootstrap Contract

Every newly provisioned internal agent receives:

```text
MCP_GATEWAY_URL=https://mcp.miam.home.arpa/mcp
MCP_AGENT_TOKEN=<agent-scoped token>
ACMS_AGENT_ID=<durable ACMS UUID>
ACMS_AGENT_NAME=acms-hermes-worker-uid-###
```

Never print tokens.

Agent-scoped identity should be revocable and narrow.

# 13. Assignment-Scoped Token

When ACMS dispatches a Work Item, issue a short-lived:

```text
MCP_ASSIGNMENT_TOKEN
```

Scope to:

```text
specific Work UID
specific Project
specific Product if present
specific Jira issue
specific repository/repositories
specific tools/resources
specific expiration
```

Expiry removes assignment-only privileges.

# 14. Required Context Manifest

Expose a current-assignment resource such as:

```text
mcp://acms/context/current
```

Example:

```yaml
agent:
  name: acms-hermes-worker-uid-005
  id: <uuid>
  roles:
    - worker

assignment:
  work_uid: ACMS-WORK-...
  jira: STNA-88
  project: ACMS
  product: null

repositories:
  - startupteams/acms-project-framework

required_context:
  - Jira description
  - acceptance criteria
  - relevant ADRs
  - previous handoff
  - current deployment state

optional_context:
  - related artifacts
  - historical discussions

permissions:
  resources: [...]
  tools: [...]

approvals:
  required_for: [...]
```

Do not dump every document into the initial prompt.

Let the agent discover/fetch needed context.

# 15. Namespaces

Required:

```text
acms.*
llm.*
runtime.*
proxmox.*
github.*
pdu.*
power.*
jira.*
registry.*
monitoring.*
```

No vague global names such as `restart()` or `create()`.

# 16. Risk Classes

## READ

Examples:

```text
acms.work.get
llm.models.list
power.facility.get
pdu.status.get
github.repo.read
jira.issue.read
proxmox.vm.status
```

No approval.

## SAFE_WRITE

Examples:

```text
acms.work.update_progress
acms.artifact.create
jira.comment.add
github.branch.create
github.pr.create
github.comment.add
```

No human approval normally, but scoped and audited.

## SENSITIVE_WRITE

Examples:

```text
runtime.restart_self
runtime.request_migration
proxmox.sandbox.create
proxmox.vm.start_owned
proxmox.vm.stop_owned
pdu.request_reboot
jira.transition.request
```

Policy-gated and role-gated.

## DESTRUCTIVE

Examples:

```text
proxmox.vm.delete
pdu.outlet.power_off
critical service shutdown
storage deletion
```

Ordinary worker = DENY.

Executive/admin = explicit human approval unless an approved emergency policy exists.

```text
worker
reviewer

executive
infrastructure_admin
observer
```

Roles may overlap.

STEA-004 / VM906:

```text
executive
infrastructure_admin
```

Ordinary worker:

```text
worker
```

Do not require every review to use a dedicated reviewer identity.

Resources:

```text
acms.work.get
```

```text
acms.work.update_progress
acms.work.mark_blocked
```


Executive/admin can receive separate tools.

# 19. Tool Contract Metadata

Every tool defines:

```text
name
version
description
input schema
output schema
risk class
required roles
required scopes
idempotency
timeout
error types
audit behavior
side-effect class
approval policy
```

Example:

```yaml
name: acms.work.update_progress
version: 1
risk: SAFE_WRITE
roles:
  - worker
scope:
  - own_assignment
idempotency: idempotent_by_work_and_event_id
timeout: 10s
audit: true
```

# 20. Window 1 - Tiny MCP Golden Workflow

Before any broad adapter rollout:

```text
1. deploy gateway shell on VM114
2. deploy ACMS adapter
3. issue token to one worker
4. dispatch safe Work Item
5. worker reads assignment via MCP
6. worker reads Project/context via MCP
7. worker updates progress via MCP
8. worker submits handoff via MCP
9. ACMS creates artifact
10. Jira receives BLUF + exact artifact URL
```

No PDU mutation.

No Proxmox mutation.

No GitHub write yet.

# 22. Provisioning Integration

New managed agent flow:

```text
1. create durable ACMS identity
2. assign human UID/name
3. create VM/runtime
4. provision Agent Bridge
5. issue LLM Manager credential
6. issue MCP agent credential
7. configure MCP_GATEWAY_URL
8. configure ACMS_AGENT_ID
9. register runtime
10. heartbeat
11. MCP capability card appears in ACMS
```

No per-agent manual connector onboarding.

# 26. Window 4 - Proxmox / Sandbox / Deployment

Do not expose generic Proxmox root shell as the primary MCP interface.

Do not cripple agents either.

## Worker sandbox capability

Allow:

```text
proxmox.sandbox.create
proxmox.sandbox.status
proxmox.sandbox.start
proxmox.sandbox.stop
proxmox.sandbox.extend_ttl
proxmox.sandbox.destroy_own
```

Sandbox characteristics:

```text
owned by Work UID/agent
tagged
resource-limited
non-critical
non-production
automatic TTL unless extended
```

Inside its own sandbox, worker may have broad OS privileges:

```text
install packages
Docker
clone repositories
compile
test
run services
modify code
cron/systemd jobs
```

This is the middle ground between read-only and cluster root.

## Production deployment

Worker uses:

```text
proxmox.deployment.request
```

Server Manager validates:

```text
target
critical-node protection
backup
rollback
ownership/policy
resource constraints
```

Then grants a narrow deployment path.

Ordinary workers never receive generic cluster-root access.

## Protected infrastructure

Ordinary workers must not modify:

```text
MIAM00133
MIAM00147
LLDAP
PBS
critical infrastructure guests
PDU control authority
```

except through explicitly authorized domain workflows.

## Executive / infrastructure admin

STEA-004 may have broader operations.

If a rare action truly requires shell, use an audited/policy-controlled admin command request rather than a generic unaudited root-shell MCP tool.

# 31. Golden MCP Security Tests

Permanent tests must include:

```text
worker reads own Work
worker reads allowed Project
worker cannot read out-of-scope context
worker cannot restart another worker
worker cannot create new worker
worker cannot delete VM
worker cannot power off PDU
worker can create own sandbox
worker can install packages in own sandbox
worker can create own GitHub branch
worker cannot write another active agent's branch
executive can request new runtime
expired assignment token fails
revoked agent token fails
duplicate tool call is idempotent/safe
all writes generate audit
MIAM00133 denied to ordinary worker
MIAM00147 denied to ordinary worker
```

# 32. Context Isolation

Internal managed agents may receive broad company-internal context subject to role and assignment scope.

External/customer agents are OUT OF SCOPE for this gateway.

Every MCP access decision must know:

```text
agent
roles
assignment
Project/Product
scope
```

# 33. Gateway Failure Behavior

Gateway failure must not break existing infrastructure.

If gateway fails:

```text
ACMS REST still works
A2A still works
SSE still works
LLM Manager still works
PDU Manager still works
```

Agents should report `MCP unavailable`, not infer that resources do not exist.

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Jira kickoff linkage + reconciliation tables (window-5 plan §7/§13).\n2|\n3|Four concerns, all durable (no in-memory state survives restarts):\n4|\n5|1. Never mutated; the\n10|   newest row is the last observation.\n11|\n12|3. ``work_runtime_holds`` — persistent local PAUSE/STOP controls (§13.3).\n13|   A hold survives process restarts and polls; only an explicit operator clear\n14|   (``cleared_at``) removes it. Pausing execution must not disable Jira\n15|   reconciliation — holds are honored by the gate, not by hiding the issue.\n16|\n17|4. ``jira_reconciliation_runs`` — durable ledger for every reconciliation run\n18|   (manual \"Check Jira now\", scheduled poll, bootstrap start request — all go\n19|   through ONE service). A Work Item may be dispatched to AI\n4|execution ONLY when:\n5|\n6|  1. it carries a Jira linkage (site + immutable issue id + key), and\n7|  2. the linked issue's CURRENT observed status is a configured READY state\n8|     (default ``TO START`` — project workflow metadata is verified at runtime,\n9|     never assumed globally valid), and\n10|  3. the issue is CURRENTLY assigned to the dedicated AI account\n11|     (startupteamscompany@gmail.com — resolved to a Jira accountId, matched by\n12|     accountId, never by display name or email visibility), and\n13|  4. no local hold (pause/stop) or operator restart authorization is pending.\n14|\n15|Eligibility is NECESSARY but never sufficient: budgets, runtime health, and\n16|local holds still apply after this gate (dispatch order: Jira → budget → …).\n17|\n18|Server-side enforcement: dispatch_service calls :func:`gate_work_item` on every\n19|path (UI, API, scheduler, retry, resume, Executive decomposition, product\n20|bootstrap). Drafts may be saved unlinked; they are visibly non-executable.\n21|\n22|Design notes:\n23|- The gate NEVER mutates Jira. Observation is read-only; transitions are the\n24|  reconciliation service's concern.\n25|- Local holds are durable DB rows — a process restart cannot clear them and a\n26|  poll cannot override them (plan §13.3).\n27|- One Jira issue may authorize multiple ACMS Work Items (§7.3) — descendants\n28|  inherit the authorization; the gate checks the TOP-LEVEL linkage up the\n29|  parent chain as well as the item's own linkage.\n30|- Observation staleness: eligibility is verified at dispatch time with a LIVE\n31|  Jira read when credentials are configured (bounded timeout).             f\"started={counts.started} paused={counts.paused} stopped={counts.stopped} \"\n263|                 f\"unchanged={counts.unchanged} failed={counts.failed}\"),\n264|        metadata={\"run_id\": run.run_id, **counts.to_dict(),\n265|                  \"scan_completeness\": scan_completeness},\n266|    )\n267|    await db.commit()\n268|    return issue.key\n279|    issue_id = str(getattr(issue, \"issue_id\", \"\") or \"\")\n280|    status_u = (issue.status or \"\").strip().upper()\n281|    assignee_account = return\n287|    seen_links.add(dedupe_id)\n288|\n289|    # find authorization link (for candidate-search-sourced issues)\n290|    if link is None and issue_id:\n291|        link = (await db.scalars(\n292|            select(JiraIssueLinkRecord).where(JiraIssueLinkRecord.jira_issue_id == issue_id)\n293|        )).first()\n294|\n295|    # append-only observation\n296|    await the candidate is RECORDED (event) so the UI\n303|        # can offer import. Auto-start applies only to ALREADY-LINKED work.\n304|        if status_u in ready_statuses():\n305|            counts.unchanged += 1\n306|            await add_event(\n307|                db, event_type=\"JIRA_CANDIDATE_OBSERVED\", actor_source=\"jira_reconcile\",\n308|                summary=f\"ready candidate {key} observed (not yet linked to ACMS work); reconcile human final disposition; never fabricate\n338|        await _apply_stop(db, work, link, run, counts,\n339|                          reason=f\"Jira {key} → {issue.status} (human final disposition)\",\n340|                          soft=True)\n341|    elif not assigned_to_ai:\n342|        await _apply_pause(db, work, link, run, counts,\n343|                           reason=f\"Jira {key} assignment withdrawn from AI account\",\n344|                           hold_kind=HOLD_PAUSE)\n345|    elif status_u in PAUSE_STATUS_NAMES:\n346|        await _apply_pause(db, work, link, run, counts,\n347|                           reason=f\"Jira {key} → {issue.status}\")\n348|    elif status_u in IN_REVIEW_STATUS_NAMES:\n349|        # stop launching implementation; checkpoint into review-pending\n350|        counts.unchanged += 1\n351|        await add_event(db, event_type=\"JIRA_WORK_IN_REVIEW\", actor_source=\"jira_reconcile\",\n352|                        work_key=work.work_key,\n353|                        summary=f\"Jira {key} in review — no new implementation dispatches; \"\n354|                                f\"await human review or new ready transition\",\n355|                        metadata={\"issue_key\": key, \"run_id\": run.run_id})\n356|    elif status_u in IN_PROGRESS_STATUS_NAMES:\n357|        if not _has_proven_ready_generation(link):\n358|            # In Progress with NO proven ready kickoff → Attention; START is a separate explicit\n395|      step (start_request) so bootstraps/imports choose their own moment —\n396|      EXCEPT when the work item is PLANNED and the authorization is fresh,\n397|      where §13.2 row 1 says: import/correlate, plan, queue/start once gates\n398|      pass. Queueing = marking ready-to-start.\n424|        link.last_generation_started_at = None  # start recorded by dispatch gate\n425|        counts.added += 1\n426|        await add_event(db, event_type=\"JIRA_WORK_QUEUED\", actor_source=\"jira_reconcile\",\n427|                        work_key=work.work_key,\n428|                        summary=f\"Jira {issue.key} ready+assigned → work {work.work_key or work.work_item_id[:8]} \"\n429|                                f\"queued for gated start\",\n430|                        metadata={\"issue_key\": issue.key, \"run_id\": run.run_id,\n431|                                  \"generation\": (link.generation_count or 0) + 1})\n432|    else:\n433|        counts.unchanged += 1\n434|\n435|\n436|async def _apply_pause(db: AsyncSession, work: WorkItemRecord, link: JiraIssueLinkRecord,\n437|                       run: JiraReconciliationRunRecord, counts: ReconcileCounts, *,\n438|                       reason: str, work_sequence=seq,\n66|        parent_id=payload.parent_id,\n67|        kind=payload.kind.value,\n68|        title=payload.title,\n69|        created_by=payload.created_by,\n70|        project_id=payload.project_id,\n71|        status=WorkItemStatus.PLANNED.value,\n72|        scope_markdown=payload.scope_markdown,\n73|        created_at=now,\n74|        updated_at=now,\n75| db.scalars(select(WorkItemRecord).order_by(WorkItemRecord.created_at))\n88|    return list(result.all())\n89|\n90|\n91|async def update_work_item(\n92|    db: AsyncSession, work_item_id: str, payload: None:\n98|        record.status = payload.status.value\n99|    if payload.title is not None:\n100|        record.title = payload.title\n101|    if payload.scope_markdown is not None:\n102|        record.scope_markdown = payload.scope_markdown\n103|    if payload.project_id is not None:\n104|        from .a2a_models import ProjectRecord\n105|\n106|        project = await db.get(ProjectRecord, record.updated_at = _now()\n111|    await db.commit()\n112|    await db.refresh(record)\n113|    return record\n114|\n115|\n116|# ---------------------------------------------------------------- assignments\n117|\n118|\n119|async def assign_primary(\n120|    db: AsyncSession, payload: AssignmentCreate, assigned_by: str | None = None\n121|) -> tuple[AssignmentRecord | None, str | None]:\n122| | 'unknown-work-item' | 'work-item-not-open' |\n126|    'agent-has-active-assignment'.\n127|    \"\"\"\n128|    agent = await db.get(AgentRecord, payload.agent_id)\n129|    if agent is None:\n130| db.scalars(\n139|            select(AssignmentRecord).where(\n140|                AssignmentRecord.agent_id == payload.agent_id,\n141|                AssignmentRecord.status == \"ACTIVE\",\n142| {"content": "1|\"\"\"A2A work dispatch — the single authoritative path for starting NEW cloud\n2|execution of a Work Item on a worker agent (REV2 Phase C, REQ-059 gate).\n3|\n4|Design rules from the plan:\n5|- ONE dispatch point. UI/API layers call this service; budget checks are never\n6|  duplicated in several callers.\n7|- Gate BEFORE new cloud execution: budget execution-check verdict decides.\n8|  - below hard limit  → dispatch\n9|  - soft exceeded     → dispatch + retain the warning in the event trail\n10|  - hard exceeded     → no NEW dispatch; durable event + Attention item\n11|                        (EXECUTION_REJECTED); in-flight work untouched\n12|  - hard + override   → dispatch + audited override trail already exists\n13|- Unknown cost stays UNKNOWN — never a fabricated zero (the budget engine\n14|  itself never blocks on unknown cost; the dispatch path preserves the fact).\n15|- Idempotency: a client-supplied idempotency key (or a stable hash of the\n16|  work/assignment pair) maps to ONE execution task; retries return the\n17|  original task instead of dispatching twice.\n18|- Correlation recorded on the task + event: Work ID/key, assignment, bridge\n19|  run id, budget-check verdict (audit trail), external A2A task id.\n20|\"\"\"\n21|from __future__ import annotations\n22|\n23|import hashlib\n24|import json\n25|from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n26|from typing import Any\n27|\n28|from sqlalchemy import select\n29|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n30|\n31|from . import budget_service, work_service\n32|from .session_lifecycle import fail_session_for_dispatch, open_session_for_dispatch\n33|from .bridge import BridgeError, get_bridge_for_agent\n34|from .telemetry_models import AgentEventRecord\n35|from .telemetry_service import add_event\n36|from .work_models import ExecutionTaskCreate, ExecutionTaskRecord, WorkItemRecord\n37|\n38|\n39|ASSIGNMENT_SCHEMA_VERSION = \"acms-a2a-assignment-v1\"\n40|\n41|\n42|class DispatchError(Exception):\n43|    \"\"\"Machine-readable dispatch failure (message = stable error code).\"\"\"\n44|\n45|    def __init__(self, code: str, http_status: int = 409, detail: dict | None = None):\n46|        super().__init__(code)\n47|        self.code = code\n48|        self.http_status = http_status\n49|        self.detail = detail or {}\n50|\n51|\n52|def _idempotency_key(work_item_id: str, assignment_id: str | None,\n53|                     client_key: str | None) -> str:\n54|    \"\"\"Stable dedupe key. Client key wins; else the open assignment; else work.\"\"\"\n55|    basis = client_key or assignment_id or work_item_id\n56|    return hashlib.sha256(f\"acms-dispatch:{basis}\".encode()).hexdigest()[:32]\n57|\n58|\n59|async def _check_worker_busy(db: AsyncSession, agent_id: str, work_item_id: str) -> dict | None:\n60|    \"\"\"One task per worker (plan §3; ADR-0013): refuse a NEW dispatch when the\n61|    target agent already has a non-terminal execution task for a DIFFERENT\n62|    work item. Returns the blocking detail or None.\"\"\"\n63|    rows = (await db.scalars(\n64|        select(ExecutionTaskRecord)\n65|        .where(ExecutionTaskRecord.agent_id == agent_id,\n66|               ExecutionTaskRecord.status == \"RUNNING\")\n67|        .order_by(ExecutionTaskRecord.started_at.desc())\n68|        .limit(10)\n69|    )).all()\n70|    for t in rows:\n71|        if t.work_item_id != work_item_id:\n72|            return {\n73|                \"blocking_work_item_id\": t.work_item_id,\n74|                \"blocking_task_id\": t.task_id,\n75|                \"blocking_since\": t.started_at.isoformat() if t.started_at else None,\n76|            }\n77|    return None\n78|\n79|\n80|def _build_assignment_envelope(\n81|    *, work: WorkItemRecord, agent_id: str, task_id: str, assignment_id: str | None,\n82|    session_id: str | None, jira_issue_key: str | None, project: dict | None,\n83|    product: dict | None, sprint: dict | None, tags: list[str],\n84|    instruction: str, completion_condition: str | None,\n85|    output_artifacts_expected: list[str], model_policy: dict, priority: str,\n86|    callback_base: str, callback_token_hint: str, idempotency_key: str,\n87|) -> dict[str, Any]:\n88|    \"\"\"acms-a2a-assignment-v1 envelope (ADR-0013; plan §12). project may never\n89|    be null; product/sprint may be.\"\"\"\n90|    return {\n91|        \"schema_version\": ASSIGNMENT_SCHEMA_VERSION,\n92|        \"assignment_id\": assignment_id,\n93|        \"idempotency_key\": idempotency_key,\n94|        \"agent_id\": str(agent_id),\n95|        \"work_item_id\": work.work_item_id,\n96|        \"execution_task_id\": task_id,\n97|        \"execution_session_id\": session_id,\n98|        \"work_key\": work.work_key,\n99|        \"jira_issue_key\": jira_issue_key,\n100|        \"project\": project,\n101|        \"product\": product,\n102|        \"sprint\": sprint,\n103|        \"tags\": tags,\n104|        \"instructions\": instruction,\n105|        \"completion_condition\": completion_condition,\n106|        \"output_artifacts_expected\": output_artifacts_expected,\n107|        \"model_policy\": model_policy,\n108|        \"priority\": priority,\n109|        \"callback_urls\": {\n110|            \"heartbeat\": f\"{callback_base}/api/v1/fleet/agents/{agent_id}/heartbeat\" if callback_base else None,\n111|            \"events\": f\"{callback_base}/api/v1/execution/{task_id}/stream\" if callback_base else None,\n112|            \"completion\": f\"{callback_base}/api/v1/callbacks/execution-completion\" if callback_base else None,\n113|            \"artifacts\": f\"{callback_base}/api/v1/artifacts\" if callback_base else None,\n114|            \"human_attention\": f\"{callback_base}/api/v1/inbox\" if callback_base else None,\n115|        },\n116|        \"callback_token_hint\": callback_token_hint,\n117|        \"created_at\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n118|    }\n119|\n120|\n121|async def _find_existing_task(db: AsyncSession, dedupe_key: str) -> Any | None:\n122|    \"\"\"The most recent task created from this dedupe key.\n123|\n124|    The dedupe ledger lives in the durable EXECUTION_DISPATCHED event metadata\n125|    (``dedupe_key`` → ``task_id``) — external_task_id now carries the A2A run\n126|    id (ADR-0012 callback binding), so it can no longer double as the idem-\n127|    potency ledger. The event log is the same durability class ACMS already\n128|    relies on for reassignment counting (PR #32 pattern).\n129|    \"\"\"\n130|    rows = (await db.scalars(\n131|        select(AgentEventRecord)\n132|        .where(AgentEventRecord.event_type == \"EXECUTION_DISPATCHED\")\n133|        .order_by(AgentEventRecord.timestamp.desc())\n134|        .limit(200)\n135|    )).all()\n136|    for ev in rows:  # ---- 2a. one-task-per-worker (plan §3; ADR-0013) ------------------------\n183|    busy = await _check_worker_busy(db, str(target_agent), work_item_id)\n184|    if busy is not None:\n185|        await add_event(\n186|            db,\n187|            event_type=\"EXECUTION_REJECTED\",\n188|            actor_source=\"dispatch\",\n189|            agent_id=target_agent,\n190|            work_key=work.work_key,\n191|            assignment_key=active.assignment_key if active else None,\n192|            summary=(f\"409 WORKER_BUSY: {str(target_agent)[:8]} already executes \"\n193|                     f\"{busy['blocking_work_item_id'][:8]} — scheduler must pick \"\n194|                     f\"another idle worker\")[:512],\n195|            metadata={\"worker_busy\": busy, \"work_item_id\": work_item_id,\n196|                      \"dedupe_key\": dedupe_key},\n197|        )\n198|        await db.commit()\n199|        return {\"dispatched\": False, \"reason\": \"worker_busy\", \"worker_busy\": busy,\n200|                \"task_id\": None, \"external_task_id\": None, \"budget_check\": None}\n201|\n202|    # ---- 2b. {"content": "1|\"\"\"ADR-0012 completion callback API — versioned, scoped-token, fail-closed.\n2|\n3|Auth model: this endpoint uses a DEDICATED callback token\n4|(``ACMS_CALLBACK_TOKEN``) rather than the admin token — a worker runtime only\n5|needs the ability to report its own terminal results, not admin authority.\n6|Unset token ⇒ 503 fail-closed (endpoint effectively disabled, matching the\n7|session-secret pattern).\n8|\"\"\"\n9|from __future__ import annotations\n10|\n11|from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header, HTTPException, Request, status\n12|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n13|\n14|from .completion_callback import (\n15|    CompletionCallbackError,\n16|    CompletionCallbackRequest,\n17|    process_completion_callback,\n18|)\n19|from .db import get_session\n20|from .settings import get_settings\n21|\n22|router = APIRouter(prefix=\"/api/v1/callbacks\", dependencies=[])\n23|\n24|\n25|def _require_callback_token(authorization: str | None = Header(default=None)) -> None:\n26|    expected = get_settings().callback_token\n27|    if not expected:\n28|        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,\n29|                            detail=\"ACMS_CALLBACK_TOKEN not configured\")\n30|    if not authorization or not authorization.startswith(\"Bearer \"):\n31|        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail=\"Missing bearer token\")\n32|    if authorization.removeprefix(\"Bearer \") != expected:\n33|        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail=\"Invalid callback token\")\n34|\n35|\n36|class CompletionCallbackResponse(dict):\n37|    pass\n38|\n39|\n40|@router.post(\"/execution-completion\", dependencies=[Depends(_require_callback_token)])\n41|async def execution_completion(\n42|    payload: CompletionCallbackRequest,\n43|    request: Request,\n44|    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),\n45|) -> dict:\n46|    \"\"\"Terminal-result delivery from the worker runtime (ADR-0012 primary path).\n47|\n48|    - 200 ``result=closed`` → task + session atomically closed, semantic event\n49|      emitted (EXECUTION_COMPLETED / EXECUTION_FAILED / EXECUTION_CANCELLED).\n50|    - 200 ``result=already_terminal`` → idempotent replay (duplicate/late\n51|      callback) — harmless by design.\n52|    - 404 unknown-task-id / 403 agent-mismatch → durable\n53|      EXECUTION_CALLBACK_REJECTED audit event.\n54|    \"\"\"\n55|    try:\n56|        result = await process_completion_callback(db, payload)\n57|    except CompletionCallbackError as e:\n58|        raise HTTPException(status_code=e.http_status,\n59|                            detail={\"code\": e.code, **e.detail}) from None\n60|    return result", "total_lines": 59, "file_size": 2475, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"ADR-0012 — worker completion callback (Accepted 2026-09-29).\n2|\n3|Canonical completion architecture:\n4|    Bridge/worker completion callback = PRIMARY immediate completion signal\n5|    ACMS reconciliation sweep          = FALLBACK / repair mechanism\n6|\n7|A callback carries durable semantic data only:\n8|    task/run id, terminal status (SUCCEEDED|FAILED|CANCELLED),\n9|    completed_at, result/handoff reference, usage/cost references when\n10|    available, concise error summary when failed.\n11|It NEVER carries full transcripts / token streams / verbose reasoning.\n12|\n13|Identity binding (fail-closed): the callback must present\n14|    acms_agent_id + execution task id (+ a2a run id when known)\n15|and the recorded task must already belong to that agent. A runtime cannot\n16|close another runtime's task. Unknown task ids are REJECTED with an audit\n17|event; wrong agent identity fails CLOSED with an audit event.\n18|\n19|Idempotency/authority:\n20|    duplicate callback        → harmless (idempotent success, no dup close)\n21|    late callback after close → harmless (idempotent)\n22|    unknown task id           → 404 + audit event\n23|    wrong agent/runtime       → 403 fail-closed + audit event\n24|    lost callback             → reconciliation eventually repairs state\n25|\"\"\"\n26|from __future__ import annotations\n27|\n28|from datetime import datetime, timezone\n29|from typing import Any\n30|\n31|from pydantic import BaseModel, Field\n32|from sqlalchemy import select\n33|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n34|\n35|from .memory_models import ExecutionSessionRecord\n36|from .telemetry_service import add_event\n37|from .work_models import ExecutionTaskRecord\n38|\n39|TERMINAL_STATUSES = (\"SUCCEEDED\", \"FAILED\", \"CANCELLED\")\n40|\n41|\n42|class CompletionCallbackError(Exception):\n43|    \"\"\"Machine-readable callback failure (message = stable error code).\"\"\"\n44|\n45|    def __init__(self, code: str, http_status: int = 409, detail: dict | None = None):\n46|        super().__init__(code)\n47|        self.code = code\n48|        self.http_status = http_status\n49|        self.detail = detail or {}\n50|\n51|\n52|class CompletionCallbackRequest(BaseModel):\n53|    \"\"\"Durable semantic payload only — never transcripts or token streams.\"\"\"\n54|\n55|    task_id: str = Field(max_length=64)\n56|    acms_agent_id: str = Field(max_length=36)\n57|    a2a_run_id: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=255)\n58|    status: str = Field(max_length=16)\n59|    completed_at: datetime | None = None\n60|    result_reference: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=512)\n61|    handoff_reference: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=512)\n62|    error_summary: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=512)\n63|    usage_reference: dict | None = None\n64|\n65|\n66|async def process_completion_callback(db: AsyncSession, payload: CompletionCallbackRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:\n67|    \"\"\"Validate identity, close the task + session atomically, audit the fact.\n68|\n69|    Returns a dict with ``result`` in {closed, already_terminal} — both are\n70|    idempotent successes. Raises CompletionCallbackError for structural\n71|    failures (unknown task, identity mismatch, invalid status).\n72|    \"\"\"\n73|    if payload.status not in TERMINAL_STATUSES:\n74|        raise CompletionCallbackError(\"invalid-status\", 422,\n75|                                      {\"allowed\": list(TERMINAL_STATUSES)})\n76|\n77|    task = await db.get(ExecutionTaskRecord, payload.task_id)\n78|    if task is None:\n79|        # Unknown task id: reject + audit (ADR-0012 §1.8). No task row to\n80|        # correlate to, so the audit event carries the claimed ids only.\n81|        await add_event(\n82|            db, event_type=\"EXECUTION_CALLBACK_REJECTED\", actor_source=\"completion_callback\",\n83|            agent_id=None, work_key=None,\n84|            summary=f\"Completion callback rejected: unknown task id {payload.task_id[:24]}\",\n85|            metadata={\"claimed_agent_id\": payload.acms_agent_id,\n86|                      \"a2a_run_id\": payload.a2a_run_id, \"reason\": \"unknown-task-id\"},\n87|        )\n88|        await db.commit()\n89|        raise CompletionCallbackError(\"unknown-task-id\", 404)\n90|\n91|    # ---- identity binding: fail closed on agent mismatch --------------------\n92|    task_agent = task.agent_id or \"\"\n93|    if task_agent != payload.acms_agent_id:\n94|        await add_event(\n95|            db, event_type=\"EXECUTION_CALLBACK_REJECTED\", actor_source=\"completion_callback\",\n96|            agent_id=task.agent_id, work_key=None,\n97|            summary=\"Completion callback rejected: agent identity mismatch (fail closed)\",\n98|            metadata={\"task_id\": payload.task_id, \"recorded_agent_id\": task_agent,\n99|                      \"claimed_agent_id\": payload.acms_agent_id, \"reason\": \"agent-mismatch\"},\n100|        )\n101|        await db.commit()\n102|        raise CompletionCallbackError(\"agent-mismatch\", 403,\n103|                                      {\"task_agent_id\": task_agent or None})\n104|\n105|    # ---- already terminal? idempotent no-op ---------------------------------\n106|    if task.status != \"RUNNING\":\n107|        return {\"result\": \"already_terminal\", \"task_id\": task.task_id,\n108|                \"task_status\": task.status, \"session_id\": None}\n109|\n110|    completed_at = payload.completed_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n111|    task.status = payload.status\n112|    task.finished_at = completed_at\n113|\n114|    # ---- close the correlated ExecutionSession (no manual close) -----------\n115|    session = (await db.scalars(\n116|        select(ExecutionSessionRecord)\n117|        .where(ExecutionSessionRecord.a2a_task_id == (payload.a2a_run_id or task.external_task_id or \"\"))\n118|        .where(ExecutionSessionRecord.status == \"OPEN\")\n119|        .order_by(ExecutionSessionRecord.started_at.desc())\n120|        .limit(1)\n121|    )).first()\n122|    if session is None and task.external_task_id:\n123|        session = (await db.scalars(\n124|            select(ExecutionSessionRecord)\n125|            .where(ExecutionSessionRecord.a2a_task_id == task.external_task_id)\n126|            .where(ExecutionSessionRecord.status == \"OPEN\")\n127|            .order_by(ExecutionSessionRecord.started_at.desc())\n128|            .limit(1)\n129|        )).first()\n130|\n131|    session_id = None\n132|    if session is not None:\n133|        session.status = \"CLOSED\"\n134|        session.ended_at = completed_at\n135|        if payload.result_reference:\n136|            session.handoff_id = None  # result/handoff refs live in the event metadata\n137|        session_id = session.session_id\n138|\n139|    # ---- durable semantic event (Attention policy applied downstream) ------\n140|    event_type = {\n141|        \"SUCCEEDED\": \"EXECUTION_COMPLETED\",\n142|        \"FAILED\": \"EXECUTION_FAILED\",\n143|        \"CANCELLED\": \"EXECUTION_CANCELLED\",\n144|    }[payload.status]\n145|    summary_bits = [f\"Completion callback: task {payload.task_id[:12]} → {payload.status}\"]\n146|    if payload.error_summary:\n147|        summary_bits.append(f\"error: {payload.error_summary[:120]}\")\n148|    await add_event(\n149|        db, event_type=event_type, actor_source=\"completion_callback\",\n150|        agent_id=task.agent_id, work_key=None,\n151|        a2a_task_id=payload.a2a_run_id,\n152|        summary=\"; \".join(summary_bits)[:512],\n153|        metadata={\n154|            \"task_id\": payload.task_id, \"status\": payload.status,\n155|            \"session_id\": session_id, \"source\": \"adr0012_callback\",\n156|            \"result_reference\": payload.result_reference,\n157|            \"handoff_reference\": payload.handoff_reference,\n158|            \"usage_reference\": payload.usage_reference,\n159|            \"error_summary\": payload.error_summary,\n160|        },\n161|    )\n162|    # ---- Inbox auto-conversion (STEA-004 plan §23) + usage capture (§26) ----\n163|    # When the execution resolves, unresolved ACTION_REQUIRED inbox items for\n164|    # this work item demote to STALE (action became irrelevant), and the run's\n165|    # token usage is recorded for cost attribution.\n166|    try:\n167|        from .inbox_service import demote_for_work_item\n168|\n169|        await demote_for_work_item(db, task.work_item_id,\n170|                                   to_class=\"STALE\",\n171|                                   reason=f\"execution {payload.status.lower()}\")\n172|    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — inbox hygiene never breaks completion\n173|        pass\n174|    try:\n175|        from .economics_service import record_execution_usage\n176|\n177|        await record_execution_usage(\n178|            db, task=task, session_id=session_id,\n179|            usage=payload.usage_reference or {}, completed_at=completed_at)\n180|    except ImportError:\n181|        pass  # economics usage capture ships with the §26 pipeline\n182|    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — usage capture never breaks completion\n183|        pass\n184|\n185|    # ---- canonical work handoff (STEA-004 plan §9) ---------------------------\n186|    # Every terminal Work Item gets exactly one canonical Markdown handoff.\n187|    # Never breaks completion (swallows + audits internally).\n188|    try:\n189|        from .canonical_handoff import generate_canonical_handoff\n190|\n191|        await generate_canonical_handoff(\n192|            db, task=task, status=payload.status, completed_at=completed_at,\n193|            error_summary=payload.error_summary,\n194|            usage_reference=payload.usage_reference,\n195|            handoff_reference=payload.handoff_reference,\n196|        )\n197|    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — belt & braces around the never-raise helper\n198|        pass\n199|    await db.commit()\n200|    return {\"result\": \"closed\", \"task_id\": task.task_id,\n201|            \"task_status\": task.status, \"session_id\": session_id}", "total_lines": 200, "file_size": 9116, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Canonical Markdown work handoff per completed Work Item (STEA-004 plan §9).\n2|\n3|Rule: the Inbox is NOT the handoff. Every terminal Work Item gets exactly one\n4|canonical ``work_handoff`` artifact:\n5|\n6|- If the agent provided handoff content (work_handoffs table OR the callback's\n7|  handoff_reference), it becomes the canonical body — the agent's output is\n8|  NEVER overwritten (plan §47: artifact generation never overwrites original\n9|  agent output).\n10|- Otherwise ACMS synthesizes a concise fallback from durable data: work item,\n11|  events trail, execution sessions (model/tokens/cost), artifacts, Jira links.\n12|\n13|Idempotent: one canonical handoff per work item (lookup before create); a\n14|second completion signal never duplicates it. Handoff generation NEVER breaks\n15|completion — every failure is swallowed with a durable audit event.\n16|\n17|Sections follow plan §9 exactly:\n18|    BLUF / Requested Work / What Was Done / Result / Tests-Evidence /\n19|    Model-Agent-Runtime / Artifacts-Code-PRs / Jira / Costs-Usage /\n20|    Problems-Warnings / Remaining Work\n21|\"\"\"\n22|from __future__ import annotations\n23|\n24|import json\n25|from datetime import datetime, timezone\n26|\n27|from sqlalchemy import select\n28|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n29|\n30|from .uid_keys import allocate_artifact_uid\n31|\n32|HANDOFF_TYPE = \"work_handoff\"\n33|\n34|\n35|def _now() -> datetime:\n36|    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n37|\n38|\n39|def _fmt_ts(dt: datetime | None) -> str:\n40|    if dt is None:\n41|        return \"—\"\n42|    if dt.tzinfo is None:\n43|        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n44|    return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC\")\n45|\n46|\n47|def _safe_json(raw) -> dict:\n48|    if isinstance(raw, dict):\n49|        return raw\n50|    if isinstance(raw, str) and raw:\n51|        try:\n52|            v = json.loads(raw)\n53|            return v if isinstance(v, dict) else {}\n54|        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n55|            return {}\n56|    return {}\n57|\n58|\n59|async def _agent_handoff_body(db: AsyncSession, work_item_id: str) -> str | None:\n60|    \"\"\"Most recent structured agent handoff for this work item, if any.\"\"\"\n61|    from .work_models import HandoffRecord\n62|\n63|    row = (await db.scalars(\n64|        select(HandoffRecord)\n65|        .where(HandoffRecord.work_item_id == work_item_id)\n66|        .order_by(HandoffRecord.created_at.desc())\n67|        .limit(1)\n68|    )).first()\n69|    return (row.body_markdown or \"\").strip() or None if row else None\n70|\n71|\n72|async def canonical_handoff_exists(db: AsyncSession, work_item_id: str) -> bool:\n73|    from .a2a_models import ArtifactRecord\n74|\n75|    return (await db.scalars(\n76|        select(ArtifactRecord.artifact_id)\n77|        .where(ArtifactRecord.work_item_id == work_item_id)\n78|        .where(ArtifactRecord.artifact_type == HANDOFF_TYPE)\n79|        .limit(1)\n80|    )).first() is not None\n81|\n82|\n83|async def generate_canonical_handoff(\n84|    db: AsyncSession,\n85|    *,\n86|    task,\n87|    status: str,\n88|    completed_at: datetime | None = None,\n89|    error_summary: str | None = None,\n90|    usage_reference: dict | None = None,\n91|    handoff_reference: str | None = None,\n92|) -> dict | None:\n93|    \"\"\"Create the canonical work_handoff artifact for a terminal work item.\n94|\n95|    Returns {artifact_id, artifact_uid, reused} or None when generation is\n96|    skipped (no work item, already exists) — never raises.\n97|    \"\"\"\n98|    try:\n99|        work_item_id = getattr(task, \"work_item_id\", None)\n100|        if not work_item_id:\n101|            return None  # standalone task with no Work Item: no handoff target\n102|\n103|        from .a2a_models import ArtifactRecord\n104|        from .work_models import WorkItemRecord\n105|\n106|        work = await db.get(WorkItemRecord, work_item_id)\n107|        if work is None:\n108|            return None\n109|        if await canonical_handoff_exists(db, work_item_id):\n110|            return {\"result\": \"already_exists\", \"work_item_id\": work_item_id}\n111|\n112|        completed_at = completed_at or _now()\n113|        agent_id = getattr(task, \"agent_id\", None)\n114|\n115|        # ---- gather durable context ------------------------------------------\n116|        from .memory_models import ExecutionSessionRecord as _ESR\n117|\n118|        sessions = (await db.scalars(\n119|            select(_ESR)\n120|            .where(_ESR.work_item_id == work_item_id)\n121|            .order_by(_ESR.started_at.asc())\n122|        )).all()\n123|\n124|        artifacts = (await db.scalars(\n125|            select(ArtifactRecord)\n126|            .where(ArtifactRecord.work_item_id == work_item_id)\n127|            .order_by(ArtifactRecord.created_at.asc())\n128|        )).all()\n129|\n130|        agent_name = None\n131|        if agent_id:\n132|            from .models import AgentRecord\n133|\n134|            agent = await db.get(AgentRecord, agent_id)\n135|            agent_name = getattr(agent, \"display_name\", None) or getattr(agent, \"worker_uid\", None)\n136|\n137|        # ---- choose canonical body -------------------------------------------\n138|        agent_body = await _agent_handoff_body(db, work_item_id)\n139|        if agent_body:\n140|            body_source = \"agent\"\n141|            body = agent_body\n142|        else:\n143|            body_source = \"acms_fallback\"\n144|            body = _fallback_body(\n145|                work=work, status=status, completed_at=completed_at,\n146|                sessions=sessions, artifacts=artifacts, agent_name=agent_name,\n147|                error_summary=error_summary, usage_reference=usage_reference,\n148|                handoff_reference=handoff_reference,\n149|            )\n150|\n151|        bluf = _extract_bluf(body) or (\n152|            f\"Work {work.work_uid or work.work_item_id} finished with status {status}.\"\n153|        )\n154|\n155|        _seq, uid = await allocate_artifact_uid(db, completed_at)\n156|        art = ArtifactRecord(\n157|            artifact_id=ArtifactRecord.new_id(),\n158|            artifact_uid=uid, artifact_sequence=_seq,\n159|            work_item_id=work_item_id, agent_id=agent_id,\n160|            project_id=work.project_id, product_id=None,\n161|            jira_issue_key=work.jira_issue_key,\n162|            artifact_type=HANDOFF_TYPE,\n163|            title=f\"Work Handoff — {work.work_uid or work.work_item_id}\",\n164|            bluf=bluf[:512],\n165|            content=body,\n166|            sha256=ArtifactRecord.sha(body),\n167|            created_at=completed_at, created_by=f\"canonical-handoff:{body_source}\",\n168|        )\n169|        db.add(art)\n170|\n171|        # Durable audit event (§35 traceability)\n172|        from .telemetry_service import add_event\n173|\n174|        await add_event(\n175|            db, event_type=\"WORK_HANDOFF_GENERATED\", actor_source=\"canonical_handoff\",\n176|            agent_id=agent_id, work_key=work.work_key,\n177|            summary=f\"Canonical work handoff {uid} created ({body_source})\",\n178|            metadata={\"work_item_id\": work_item_id, \"artifact_id\": art.artifact_id,\n179|                      \"artifact_uid\": uid, \"body_source\": body_source, \"status\": status},\n180|        )\n181|     pass\n195|        return None\n196|\n197|\n198|def _extract_bluf(body: str) -> str | None:\n199|    \"\"\"Pull the BLUF section's first paragraph from a Markdown body.\"\"\"\n200|    lines = body.splitlines()\n201|    in_bluf = False\n202|    buf: list[str] = []\n203|    for ln in lines:\n204|        if ln.strip().lower().startswith(\"#\") and \"bluf\" in ln.lower():\n205|            in_bluf = True\n206|            continue\n207|        if in_bluf:\n208|            if ln.strip().startswith(\"#\"):\n209|                break\n210|            if ln.strip():\n211| scope recorded)\")\n238|    parts.append(\"\")\n239|    parts.append(\"## What Was Done\")\n240|    if sessions:\n241|        for s in sessions:\n242|            bits = [f\"- Session `{s.session_id[:8]}` — status {s.status}\"]\n243|            if s.model_id:\n244|                bits.append(f\"model `{s.model_id}`\")\n245|            if s.a2a_task_id:\n246|                bits.append(f\"A2A run `{s.a2a_task_id[:24]}`\")\n247|            parts.append(\" — \".join(bits))\n248|    else:\n249|        parts.append(\"- (no execution sessions recorded)\")\n250|    parts.append(\"\")\n251|    parts.append(\"## Result\")\n252|    parts.append(f\"- Terminal status: **{status}** at {_fmt_ts(completed_at)}\")\n253|    if handoff_reference:\n254|        parts.append(f\"- Agent handoff reference: `{handoff_reference[:200]}`\")\n255|    parts.append(\"\")\n256|    parts.append(\"## Tests / Evidence\")\n257|    ev_bits = []\n258|    for a in a.artifact_id}) \"\n262|                       f\"({a.artifact_type}, sha {a.sha256[:8]})\")\n263|    parts.extend(ev_bits or [\"- (no evidence artifacts recorded)\"])\n264|    parts.append(\"\")\n265|    parts.append(\"## Model / Agent / Runtime\")\n266|    models = sorted({s.model_id for s in sessions if s.model_id})\n267|    parts.append(f\"- Agent: {agent_name or 'unknown'}\")\n268|    parts.append(f\"- Model(s): {', '.join(models) or 'UNKNOWN (never fabricated)'}\")\n269|    parts.append(\"\")\n270|    parts.append(\"## Artifacts / Code / PRs\")\n271|    if artifacts:\n272|        for a in artifacts:\n273|            if a.artifact_type == HANDOFF_TYPE:\n274|                continue\n275|            parts.append(f\"- {a.artifact_uid or a.artifact_id}: {a.title}\")\n276|    else:\n277|        parts.append(\"- (none)\")\n278|    parts.append(\"\")\n279|    parts.append(\"## Jira\")\n280|    if work.jira_issue_key:\n281|        parts.append(f\"- [{work.jira_issue_key}]({work.jira_url or '#'}\")\n282|    else:\n283|        parts.append(\"- (not linked)\")\n284|    parts.append(\"\")\n285|    parts.append(\"## Costs / Usage\")\n286|    total_in = total_out = 0\n287|    for s in sessions:\n288|        total_in += s.cumulative_api_tokens or 0\n289|    u = usage_reference or {}\n290|    if u.get(\"input_tokens\"):\n291|        total_in += ### [2026-10-02T05:26:22.473087+00:00] tool · tool: read_file

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Jira↔ACMS sync service (v1, 2026-09-29 plan §6/Phase D).\n2|\n3|Inbound:  poll assigned Jira issues → high-level ACMS work context (one work\n4|          item per Jira issue MAX; Executive decomposes further internally).\n5|Outbound: useful lifecycle summaries posted as Jira comments (started /\n6|          milestone / blocked / PR available / completed / final handoff).\n7|NEVER:    one Jira ticket per internal Work Item or task (plan §D7 invariant).\n8|\"\"\"\n9|from __future__ import annotations\n10|\n11|from .jira_client import JiraClient, JiraError\n12|from .telemetry_service import TelemetryService\n13|\n14|\n15|# Correlation: Jira key stored on the Work Item title/scope prefix.\n16|JIRA_CORRELATION_PREFIX = \"jira:\"\n17|\n18|\n19|def correlation_tag(issue_key: str) -> str:\n20|    return f\"{JIRA_CORRELATION_PREFIX}{issue_key}\"\n21|\n22|\n23|def extract_jira_key(scope_or_title: str) -> str | None:\n24|    idx = scope_or_title.find(JIRA_CORRELATION_PREFIX)\n25|    if idx < 0:\n26|        return None\n27|    rest = scope_or_title[idx + len(JIRA_CORRELATION_PREFIX):]\n28|    token = rest.split()[0].split(\"\\n\")[0].strip()\n29|    return token or None\n30|\n31|\n32|class JiraSyncService:\n33|    \"\"\"Stateless-ish orchestration over JiraClient; db session injected.\"\"\"\n34|\n35|    def __init__(self, client: JiraClient | None = None) -> None:\n36|        self.client = client or JiraClient()\n37|\n38|    async def inbound_assigned_issues(self, db) -> list[dict]:\n39|        \"\"\"Fetch assigned issues and record an inbound-sync event per issue.\n40|\n41|        Returns issue dicts for the Executive Agent to decompose. Does NOT\n42|        create Work Items automatically (Executive decides; plan §D3/D4).\n43|        \"\"\"\n44|        issues = self.client.search_assigned_issues()\n45|        out = []\n46|        for issue in issues:\n47|            await TelemetryService.record_event(\n48|                db,\n49|                event_type=\"JIRA_INBOUND_SYNCED\",\n50|                summary=f\"Jira {issue.key} assigned: {issue.summary[:120]}\",\n51|                actor_source=\"jira_sync\",\n52|                metadata={\"issue_key\": issue.key, \"status\": issue.status,\n53|                          \"priority\": issue.priority, \"url\": issue.url},\n54|            )\n55|            out.append({\n56|                \"key\": issue.key, \"summary\": issue.summary,\n57|                \"description\": issue.description, \"status\": issue.status,\n58|                \"priority\": issue.priority, \"labels\": issue.labels,\n59|                \"url\": issue.url, \"correlation_tag\": correlation_tag(issue.key),\n60|            })\n61|        return out\n62|\n63|    async def outbound_summary(self, db, *, issue_key: str, kind: str, detail: str) -> str:\n64|        \"\"\"Post one lifecycle summary comment (started/milestone/blocked/pr/completed/handoff).\"\"\"\n65|        body = f\"[ACMS Executive] {kind.upper()}: {detail}\"\n66|        comment_id = self.client.add_comment(issue_key, body)\n67|        await TelemetryService.record_event(\n68|            db,\n69|            event_type=\"JIRA_OUTBOUND_POSTED\",\n70|            summary=f\"Jira {issue_key} <- {kind}: {detail[:120]}\",\n71|            actor_source=\"jira_sync\",\n72|            metadata={\"issue_key\": issue_key, \"kind\": kind, \"comment_id\": comment_id},\n73|        )\n74|        return comment_id\n75|", "total_lines": 74, "file_size": 3080, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Work-scoped Jira UI actions (window-5 plan §13.5).\n2|\n3|- \"Check Jira now\" — real authenticated POST mutation (never a GET); shows the\n4|  durable run id + per-outcome counts; duplicate clicks coalesce server-side.\n5|- Persistent Pause/Stop controls on work detail (local holds survive polls).\n6|- Jira linkage panel on work detail (issue key/url, last observed status,\n7|  assignee, eligibility verdict + reason, last checked).\n8|\"\"\"\n9|from __future__ import annotations\n10|\n11|from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, Request\n12|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n13|\n14|from ..db import get_session\n15|from ..jira_api import (HoldRequest, LinkRequest, ReconcileRequest,\n16|                        check_jira_now, clear_hold, create_hold, link_issue)\n17|from ..jira_gate import gate_work_item\n18|from ..jira_reconcile import due_for_scheduled_poll\n19|from ..work_models import WorkItemRecord\n20|from .roles import Role\n21|from .session_auth import current_user\n22|from .work_routes import RedirectSeeOther, _require_admin\n23|\n24|router = APIRouter(prefix=\"/ui/jira\", include_in_schema=False)\n25|\n26|\n27|@router.post(\"/check-now\")\n28|async def ui_check_jira_now(\n29|    request: Request,\n30|    user=Depends(current_user),\n31|    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),\n32|):\n33|    \"\"\"Check Jira now (execution-capable action — stated in the UI).\n34|\n35|    Runs the ONE reconciliation service immediately: imports/starts eligible\n36|    work, requests pauses/stops per current Jira state. Returns to the origin\n37|    with the durable run id in the redirect.\n38|    \"\"\"\n39|    _require_admin(user)\n40|    body = ReconcileRequest(actor=f\"ui:{user.username}\")\n41|    outcome = await check_jira_now(body, db)\n42|    return RedirectSeeOther(\n43|        f\"/ui/work?jira_run={outcome.run_id}&jira_state={outcome.state}\"\n44|        + (\"&jira_coalesced=1\" if outcome.coalesced else \"\")\n45|    )\n46|\n47|\n48|@router.post(\"/work/{work_item_id}/link\")\n49|async def ui_link_jira(\n50|    work_item_id: str,\n51|    request: Request,\n52|    issue_key: str = Form(..., min_length=3, max_length=64),\n53|    user=Depends(current_user),\n54|    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),\n55|):\n56|    _require_admin(user)\n57|    body = LinkRequest(issue_key=issue_key.strip(), actor=f\"ui:{user.username}\")\n58|    try:\n59|        result = await link_issue(work_item_id, body, db)\n60|        err = None\n61|    except Exception as e:  # human-readable failure per plan §14\n62|        result = None\n63|        err = str(getattr(e, \"detail\", None) or e)[:300]\n64|    target = f\"/ui/work/{work_item_id}\"\n65|    if err:\n66|        from .work_routes import RedirectWithError\n67|\n68|        return RedirectWithError(target, f\"Jira link failed: {err}\")\n69|    gate = result[\"gate\"]\n70|    return RedirectSeeOther(f\"{target}?jira_gate={gate['verdict']}\")\n71|\n72|\n73|@router.post(\"/work/{work_item_id}/hold\")\n74|async def ui_set_hold(\n75|    work_item_id: str,\n76|    request: Request,\n77|    hold_kind: str = Form(..., pattern=\"^(PAUSE|STOP)$\"),\n78|    reason: str = Form(\"\", max_length=512),\n79|    user=Depends(current_user),\n80|    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),\n81|):\n82|    _require_admin(user)\n83|    body = HoldRequest(hold_kind=hold_kind, reason=reason, actor=f\"ui:{user.username}\")\n84|    await create_hold(work_item_id, body, db)\n85|    return RedirectSeeOther(f\"/ui/work/{work_item_id}?hold_set={hold_kind}\")\n86|\n87|\n88|@router.post(\"/work/{work_item_id}/holds/{hold_id}/clear\")\n89|async def ui_clear_hold(\n90|    work_item_id: str,\n91|    hold_id: str,\n92|    request: Request,\n93|    user=Depends(current_user),\n94|    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),\n95|):\n96|    _require_admin(user)\n97|    await clear_hold(work_item_id, hold_id, db)\n98|    return RedirectSeeOther(f\"/ui/work/{work_item_id}?hold_cleared=1\")\n99|\n100|\n101|@router.get(\"/status\")\n102|async def ui_jira_status(\n103|    request: Request,\n104|    user=Depends(current_user),\n105|    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),\n106|):\n107|    \"\"\"Small JSON for the dashboard widget: scheduler freshness + gate summary.\"\"\"\n108|    due, info = await due_for_scheduled_poll(db)\n109|    return {\"due_now\": due, **info}", "total_lines": 108, "file_size": 3876, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Widen power_cost_snapshot.payload_json to hold the full facility payload.\n2|\n3|STEA-004 Phase C live finding (2026-10-02): Release 5's fix stores channels +\n4|rate + collector detail in payload_json (latest_power_summary rebuilds the §17\n5|view from the STORED payload). The original 512-char cap truncated everything\n6|but totals, so the stored-snapshot path rendered no per-channel cards. The\n7|column becomes unbounded text; a 4KB application-side cap stays as a safety\n8|net. Fully additive/nullable — no data rewritten.\n9|\"\"\"\n10|from __future__ import annotations\n11|\n12|import sqlalchemy as sa\n13|from alembic import op\n14|\n15|revision = \"0017_power_payload_text\"\n16|down_revision = \"0016_power_snapshot\"\n17|branch_labels = None\n18|depends_on = None\n19|\n20|\n21|def upgrade() -> None:\n22|    with op.batch_alter_table(\"power_cost_snapshot\") as batch:\n23|        batch.alter_column(\"payload_json\", existing_type=sa.String(length=512),\n24|                           type_=sa.Text(), existing_nullable=True)\n25|\n26|\n27|def downgrade() -> None:\n28|    # Downgrade truncates stored payloads to 512 chars where needed (SQLite\n29|    # batch recreates the table; PG ALTER back to varchar(512) requires the\n30|    # data to fit — trim defensively first).\n31|    bind = op.get_bind()\n32|    if bind.dialect.name == \"postgresql\":\n33|        bind.execute(sa.text(\n34|            \"UPDATE power_cost_snapshot \"\n35|            \"SET payload_json = LEFT(payload_json, 512) \"\n36|            \"WHERE payload_json IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(payload_json) > 512\"))\n37|    with op.batch_alter_table(\"power_cost_snapshot\") as batch:\n38|        batch.alter_column(\"payload_json\", existing_type=sa.Text(),\n39|                           type_=sa.String(length=512), existing_nullable=True)", "total_lines": 38, "file_size": 1666, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}
